DROP TABLE IF EXISTS digests;
CREATE TABLE digests (
    id INTEGER NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    algo INTEGER NOT NULL,
    digest TEXT NOT NULL,
    is_canonical BOOLEAN NOT NULL,
    resource_id INTEGER REFERENCES resources(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    tile_id INTEGER REFERENCES tiles(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    patch_id INTEGER REFERENCES patches(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    CHECK (
        (resource_id IS NOT NULL AND tile_id IS NULL AND patch_id IS NULL) OR
        (resource_id IS NULL AND tile_id IS NOT NULL AND patch_id IS NULL) OR
        (resource_id IS NULL AND tile_id IS NULL AND patch_id IS NOT NULL)
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX tile_digests_idx ON digests (tile_id, algo, is_canonical);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX resource_digests_idx ON digests (resource_id, algo, is_canonical);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX patch_digests_idx ON digests (patch_id, algo, is_canonical);

BEGIN TRANSACTION;
CREATE TABLE variants (
    id INTEGER NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    domain INTEGER NOT NULL,
    dataset TEXT NOT NULL,
    version TEXT NOT NULL,
    UNIQUE (domain, dataset, version)
CREATE TABLE digests (
    id INTEGER NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    algo INTEGER NOT NULL,
    digest TEXT NOT NULL,
    is_canonical BOOLEAN NOT NULL,
    resource_id INTEGER REFERENCES resources(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    tile_id INTEGER REFERENCES tiles(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    patch_id INTEGER REFERENCES patches(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    CHECK (
        (resource_id IS NOT NULL AND tile_id IS NULL AND patch_id IS NULL) OR
        (resource_id IS NULL AND tile_id IS NOT NULL AND patch_id IS NULL) OR
        (resource_id IS NULL AND tile_id IS NULL AND patch_id IS NOT NULL)
CREATE TABLE tiles (
    id INTEGER NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    variant_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES variants(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    level INTEGER NOT NULL,
    x INTEGER NOT NULL,
    y INTEGER NOT NULL,
    flags INTEGER NOT NULL,
    revision INTEGER NOT NULL,
    bytes INTEGER,
    expires INTEGER,
    etag TEXT,
    encoding INTEGER,
    type INTEGER,
    content BLOB,
    extra_metadata BLOB,
    offline_ttl INTEGER,
    last_accessed INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    UNIQUE (variant_id, level, x, y)
CREATE TABLE resources (
    id INTEGER NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    url TEXT NOT NULL,
    domain INTEGER NOT NULL,
    flags INTEGER NOT NULL,
    revision INTEGER NOT NULL,
    bytes INTEGER,
    expires INTEGER,
    etag TEXT,
    encoding INTEGER,
    type INTEGER,
    content BLOB,
    extra_metadata BLOB,
    offline_ttl INTEGER,
    last_accessed INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    UNIQUE (url, domain)
CREATE TABLE patches (
    id INTEGER NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    tile_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES tiles(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    flags INTEGER NOT NULL,
    revision INTEGER NOT NULL,
    bytes INTEGER,
    expires INTEGER,
    etag TEXT,
    encoding INTEGER,
    type INTEGER,
    content BLOB,
    extra_metadata BLOB,
    offline_ttl INTEGER,
    last_accessed INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    UNIQUE (tile_id)
CREATE TABLE groups (
    id INTEGER NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    type INTEGER NOT NULL,
    name TEXT NOT NULL,
    geometry BLOB,
    tilesets BLOB,
    metadata BLOB,
    extradata TEXT,
    -- Cached _getGroup summary, maintained incrementally (avoids iterating a group's members).
    required_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    completed_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    completed_bytes INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    min_expires INTEGER,
    usable_after_expired INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    UNIQUE (type, name)
CREATE TABLE group_tiles (
    group_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES groups(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    tile_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES tiles(id),
    PRIMARY KEY (group_id, tile_id)
) WITHOUT ROWID;
CREATE TABLE group_resources (
    group_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES groups(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    resource_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES resources(id),
    PRIMARY KEY (group_id, resource_id)
) WITHOUT ROWID;
CREATE TABLE group_patches (
    group_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES groups(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    patch_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES patches(id),
    PRIMARY KEY (group_id, patch_id)
) WITHOUT ROWID;
CREATE TABLE invalidation_requests_ids (
    id INTEGER NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    request_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    UNIQUE (request_id)
CREATE TABLE group_update_times (
    group_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES groups(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    last_modified INTEGER,
    UNIQUE(group_id)
CREATE TABLE resume_data (
    data_path TEXT UNIQUE,
    bytes INTEGER,
    last_write INTEGER
CREATE TABLE integrity_check_state (
    id INTEGER NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT 1,
    cursor INTEGER NOT NULL,
    interval INTEGER NOT NULL,
    network_restriction INTEGER NOT NULL
-- Cached aggregate counters, maintained on writes so startup avoids full-table scans.
CREATE TABLE state (
    key TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,
    value INTEGER NOT NULL
INSERT INTO state (key, value) VALUES
    ('total_bytes', 0),
    ('ambient_cache_size', 0),
    ('tile_count', 0),
    ('resource_count', 0),
    ('patch_count', 0);
-- Cached distinct-tile count per domain across Offline groups, maintained in C++ (the offline tile-limit check).
CREATE TABLE group_tile_counts (
    domain INTEGER NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,
    count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
-- Triggers to delete leftover NULL tiles, resources and patches after removing them from a group, because setting group
-- elements may create a bunch of NULL placeholder elements that get filled in later when the actual data is written,
-- but if the group is deleted before the elements are filled in, they will be left as NULL entries that take up space
-- in the DB.
-- A tile, resource or patch is considered "placeholder" if its content is NULL, there is no active download for it
-- (pending flag not set), and it doesn't belong to any group.
CREATE TRIGGER delete_null_tile_after_group_tiles_delete__trigger
AFTER DELETE ON group_tiles
    EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM tiles WHERE (id = OLD.tile_id) AND (bytes IS NULL) AND ((flags & 1) = 0))
    AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM group_tiles WHERE tile_id = OLD.tile_id)
    DELETE FROM tiles WHERE id = OLD.tile_id;
CREATE TRIGGER delete_null_resource_after_group_resources_delete__trigger
AFTER DELETE ON group_resources
    EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM resources WHERE (id = OLD.resource_id) AND (bytes IS NULL) AND ((flags & 1) = 0))
    AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM group_resources WHERE resource_id = OLD.resource_id)
    DELETE FROM resources WHERE id = OLD.resource_id;
CREATE TRIGGER delete_null_patch_after_group_patches_delete__trigger
AFTER DELETE ON group_patches
    EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM patches WHERE (id = OLD.patch_id) AND (bytes IS NULL) AND ((flags & 1) = 0))
    AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM group_patches WHERE patch_id = OLD.patch_id)
    DELETE FROM patches WHERE id = OLD.patch_id;
-- Triggers to handle tile deletion when there is a patch associated with it: If the tile is deleted before the patch,
-- instead of deleting the tile entry, we null out all the tile data, making it a placeholder tile. When the patch is
-- then deleted, we have a trigger to delete the corresponding tile if it's a placeholder tile.
CREATE TRIGGER set_tile_data_to_null_on_delete_if_patch_exists__trigger
BEFORE DELETE ON tiles
WHEN EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM patches WHERE tile_id = OLD.id)
    UPDATE tiles
        SET bytes = NULL,
            expires = NULL,
            etag = NULL,
            encoding = NULL,
            type = NULL,
            content = NULL,
            extra_metadata = NULL,
            offline_ttl = NULL
        WHERE
            id = OLD.id
            AND bytes IS NOT NULL; -- Only update if it's not already a placeholder tile.
    -- Skip the deletion, we just null out the fields so that when the patch is deleted, the tile will be deleted by
    -- the other triggers if it doesn't belong to any other group and doesn't have any other patches
    SELECT RAISE(IGNORE);
CREATE TRIGGER delete_null_tile_after_patch_delete__trigger
AFTER DELETE ON patches
    EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM tiles WHERE (id = OLD.tile_id) AND (bytes IS NULL) AND ((flags & 1) = 0))
    AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM group_tiles WHERE tile_id = OLD.tile_id)
    DELETE FROM tiles WHERE id = OLD.tile_id;
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX group_tiles__tile_id__group_id ON group_tiles (tile_id, group_id);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX group_resources__resource_id__group_id ON group_resources (resource_id, group_id);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX group_patches__patch_id__group_id ON group_patches (patch_id, group_id);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX tile_digests_idx ON digests (tile_id, algo, is_canonical);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX resource_digests_idx ON digests (resource_id, algo, is_canonical);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX patch_digests_idx ON digests (patch_id, algo, is_canonical);
CREATE INDEX tiles__last_accessed__expires ON tiles (last_accessed, expires);
CREATE INDEX resources__last_accessed__expires ON resources (last_accessed, expires);
CREATE INDEX patches__last_accessed__expires ON patches (last_accessed, expires);
-- Partial indexes used by getPendingEntryDescriptions to skip the full-table scan when the
-- vast majority of rows do not have the pending bit set.
CREATE INDEX tiles__pending     ON tiles     (id) WHERE flags & 1;
CREATE INDEX resources__pending ON resources (id) WHERE flags & 1;
CREATE INDEX patches__pending   ON patches   (id) WHERE flags & 1;

CREATE TABLE state (
    key TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,
    value INTEGER NOT NULL
INSERT INTO state (key, value) VALUES
    ('total_bytes', (SELECT COALESCE(SUM(bytes), 0)
                     FROM (SELECT bytes FROM tiles UNION ALL SELECT bytes FROM resources
                           UNION ALL SELECT bytes FROM patches))),
    ('tile_count', (SELECT COUNT(id) FROM tiles WHERE bytes IS NOT NULL)),
    ('resource_count', (SELECT COUNT(id) FROM resources WHERE bytes IS NOT NULL)),
    ('patch_count', (SELECT COUNT(id) FROM patches WHERE bytes IS NOT NULL)),
    ('ambient_cache_size', (SELECT COALESCE(SUM(bytes), 0) FROM (
        SELECT bytes FROM resources WHERE resources.id NOT IN (SELECT resource_id FROM group_resources)
        UNION ALL
        SELECT bytes FROM tiles WHERE tiles.id NOT IN (SELECT tile_id FROM group_tiles)
        UNION ALL
        SELECT patches.bytes FROM patches WHERE patches.id NOT IN (SELECT patch_id FROM group_patches)
    )));
-- Backfill the _getGroup summary columns. flags bits: immutable=4, mustRevalidate=8, status=(flags>>4)&15 [Available=0, NotFound=1], invalidated=256, serveable=512.
ALTER TABLE groups ADD COLUMN required_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;
ALTER TABLE groups ADD COLUMN completed_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;
ALTER TABLE groups ADD COLUMN completed_bytes INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;
ALTER TABLE groups ADD COLUMN min_expires INTEGER;
ALTER TABLE groups ADD COLUMN usable_after_expired INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1;
UPDATE groups SET
    required_count = (
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM group_tiles WHERE group_id = groups.id) +
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM group_resources WHERE group_id = groups.id) +
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM group_patches WHERE group_id = groups.id)),
    completed_count = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM (
        SELECT flags, revision FROM group_tiles JOIN tiles ON tiles.id = tile_id WHERE group_id = groups.id
        UNION ALL SELECT flags, revision FROM group_resources JOIN resources ON resources.id = resource_id WHERE group_id = groups.id
        UNION ALL SELECT flags, revision FROM group_patches JOIN patches ON patches.id = patch_id WHERE group_id = groups.id)
        WHERE revision > 0 AND ((flags >> 4) & 15) IN (0, 1) AND ((flags & 256) = 0 OR (flags & 512) != 0)),
    completed_bytes = (SELECT COALESCE(SUM(bytes), 0) FROM (
        SELECT flags, revision, bytes FROM group_tiles JOIN tiles ON tiles.id = tile_id WHERE group_id = groups.id
        UNION ALL SELECT flags, revision, bytes FROM group_resources JOIN resources ON resources.id = resource_id WHERE group_id = groups.id
        UNION ALL SELECT flags, revision, bytes FROM group_patches JOIN patches ON patches.id = patch_id WHERE group_id = groups.id)
        WHERE revision > 0 AND ((flags >> 4) & 15) IN (0, 1) AND ((flags & 256) = 0 OR (flags & 512) != 0)),
    min_expires = (SELECT MIN(expires) FROM (
        SELECT flags, revision, expires FROM group_tiles JOIN tiles ON tiles.id = tile_id WHERE group_id = groups.id
        UNION ALL SELECT flags, revision, expires FROM group_resources JOIN resources ON resources.id = resource_id WHERE group_id = groups.id
        UNION ALL SELECT flags, revision, expires FROM group_patches JOIN patches ON patches.id = patch_id WHERE group_id = groups.id)
        WHERE revision > 0 AND (flags & 4) = 0),
    usable_after_expired = (SELECT CASE WHEN COUNT(*) = 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END FROM (
        SELECT flags, revision FROM group_tiles JOIN tiles ON tiles.id = tile_id WHERE group_id = groups.id
        UNION ALL SELECT flags, revision FROM group_resources JOIN resources ON resources.id = resource_id WHERE group_id = groups.id
        UNION ALL SELECT flags, revision FROM group_patches JOIN patches ON patches.id = patch_id WHERE group_id = groups.id)
        WHERE revision > 0 AND (flags & 8) != 0);

    domain INTEGER NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,
    count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
INSERT INTO group_tile_counts (domain, count)
SELECT v.domain, COUNT(DISTINCT t.id)
FROM group_tiles gt
JOIN groups g ON g.id = gt.group_id AND g.type = 1
JOIN tiles t ON t.id = gt.tile_id
JOIN variants v ON v.id = t.variant_id
GROUP BY v.domain;

tilestoremetadata_loadresumedataentry
tilestoremetadata_querywithfilter
        WITH domain_filter AS (
            SELECT value as domain FROM json_each(:domain_filter)
        ),
        dataset_filter AS (
            SELECT value as dataset FROM json_each(:dataset_filter)
        ),
        version_filter AS (
            SELECT value as version FROM json_each(:version_filter)
        ),
        group_filter AS (
            SELECT
                json_extract(value, '$.group_type') AS group_type,
                json_extract(value, '$.group_name') AS group_name
            FROM json_each(:group_filter)
        ),
        limited_tiles AS (
            SELECT tiles.*, variants.domain, variants.dataset, variants.version
            FROM tiles
            INNER JOIN variants ON tiles.variant_id = variants.id
            WHERE tiles.id > :cursor AND (tiles.flags & :isComplete) = :isComplete
            AND (NOT :exclude_unavailable OR (tiles.flags & :statusMask) = 0)
            AND (
                NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM domain_filter)
                OR variants.domain IN (SELECT domain FROM domain_filter)
            )
            AND (
                NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM dataset_filter)
                OR variants.dataset IN (SELECT dataset FROM dataset_filter)
            )
            AND (
                NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM version_filter)
                OR variants.version IN (SELECT version FROM version_filter)
            )
            AND (
                (NOT :groups_enabled AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM group_tiles WHERE tile_id = tiles.id))
                OR
                (:groups_enabled AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM group_filter))
                OR
                (:groups_enabled AND EXISTS (
                    SELECT 1 FROM group_tiles
                    INNER JOIN groups ON group_tiles.group_id = groups.id
                    WHERE group_tiles.tile_id = tiles.id
                    AND (
                        (:groups_type_exclusive AND groups.type NOT IN (SELECT group_type FROM group_filter))
                        OR ((groups.name, groups.type) IN (SELECT group_name, group_type FROM group_filter))
                    )
                ))
            )
            ORDER BY tiles.id
            LIMIT :max_count
        )
        SELECT limited_tiles.id,
            x,
            y,
            level,
            bytes,
            content,
            domain,
            dataset,
            version,
            revision,
            encoding,
            digests.digest as digest,
            digests.algo as algorithm,
            digests.is_canonical as is_canonical,
            COUNT(limited_tiles.id) OVER (PARTITION BY limited_tiles.id) AS digest_count
        FROM limited_tiles
        LEFT JOIN digests ON limited_tiles.id = digests.tile_id
        ORDER BY limited_tiles.id

            WITH domain_filter AS (
                SELECT value as domain FROM json_each(:domain_filter)
            ),
            dataset_filter AS (
                SELECT value as dataset FROM json_each(:dataset_filter)
            ),
            version_filter AS (
                SELECT value as version FROM json_each(:version_filter)
            ),
            group_filter AS (
                SELECT
                    json_extract(value, '$.group_type') AS group_type,
                    json_extract(value, '$.group_name') AS group_name
                FROM json_each(:group_filter)
            )
            SELECT tiles.variant_id, level, x, y
            FROM group_filter
            JOIN groups ON groups.name = group_filter.group_name AND groups.type = group_filter.group_type
            JOIN group_tiles ON group_tiles.group_id = groups.id
            JOIN tiles ON tiles.id = group_tiles.tile_id
            JOIN variants ON variants.id = tiles.variant_id
            WHERE (
                NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM domain_filter)
                OR variants.domain IN (SELECT domain FROM domain_filter)
            )
            AND (
                NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM dataset_filter)
                OR dataset IN (SELECT dataset FROM dataset_filter)
            )
            AND (
                NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM version_filter)
                OR version IN (SELECT version FROM version_filter)
            )
            AND (NOT :exclude_unavailable OR ((tiles.flags & :statusMask) = 0 AND (tiles.flags & :isComplete) = :isComplete))

CREATE TABLE group_resources (
    group_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES groups(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    resource_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES resources(id),
    PRIMARY KEY (group_id, resource_id)
) WITHOUT ROWID;
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX group_resources__resource_id__group_id ON group_resources (resource_id, group_id);
PRAGMA user_version = 7;
    PRAGMA foreign_keys = OFF; -- Temporarily disable foreign key constraints to allow for the table alteration
    CREATE TABLE new_patches (
        id INTEGER NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        tile_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES tiles(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT, -- Changed CASCADE -> RESTRICT
        flags INTEGER NOT NULL,
        revision INTEGER NOT NULL,
        bytes INTEGER,
        expires INTEGER,
        etag TEXT,
        encoding INTEGER,
        type INTEGER,
        content BLOB,
        extra_metadata BLOB,
        offline_ttl INTEGER,
        last_accessed INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        UNIQUE (tile_id)
    );
    INSERT INTO new_patches SELECT * FROM patches;
    DROP TABLE patches;
    ALTER TABLE new_patches RENAME TO patches;
    PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON; -- Re-enable foreign key constraints
    -- Recreate the indexes on the patches table to ensure they are correctly associated with the new table
    CREATE INDEX patches__last_accessed__expires ON patches (last_accessed, expires);
   -- Triggers to delete leftover NULL tiles, resources and patches
    CREATE TRIGGER delete_null_tile_after_group_tiles_delete__trigger
    AFTER DELETE ON group_tiles
    WHEN
        EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM tiles WHERE (id = OLD.tile_id) AND (bytes IS NULL) AND ((flags & 1) = 0))
        AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM group_tiles WHERE tile_id = OLD.tile_id)
    BEGIN
        DELETE FROM tiles WHERE id = OLD.tile_id;
    END;
    CREATE TRIGGER delete_null_resource_after_group_resources_delete__trigger
    AFTER DELETE ON group_resources
    WHEN
        EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM resources WHERE (id = OLD.resource_id) AND (bytes IS NULL) AND ((flags & 1) = 0))
        AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM group_resources WHERE resource_id = OLD.resource_id)
    BEGIN
        DELETE FROM resources WHERE id = OLD.resource_id;
    END;
    CREATE TRIGGER delete_null_patch_after_group_patches_delete__trigger
    AFTER DELETE ON group_patches
    WHEN
        EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM patches WHERE (id = OLD.patch_id) AND (bytes IS NULL) AND ((flags & 1) = 0))
        AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM group_patches WHERE patch_id = OLD.patch_id)
    BEGIN
        DELETE FROM patches WHERE id = OLD.patch_id;
    END;
    -- Triggers to handle tile deletion when there is a patch associated with it
    CREATE TRIGGER set_tile_data_to_null_on_delete_if_patch_exists__trigger
    BEFORE DELETE ON tiles
    WHEN EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM patches WHERE tile_id = OLD.id)
    BEGIN
        UPDATE tiles
            SET bytes = NULL,
                expires = NULL,
                etag = NULL,
                encoding = NULL,
                type = NULL,
                content = NULL,
                extra_metadata = NULL,
                offline_ttl = NULL
            WHERE
                id = OLD.id
                AND bytes IS NOT NULL;
        SELECT RAISE(IGNORE);
    END;
    CREATE TRIGGER delete_null_tile_after_patch_delete__trigger
    AFTER DELETE ON patches
    WHEN
        EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM tiles WHERE (id = OLD.tile_id) AND (bytes IS NULL) AND ((flags & 1) = 0))
        AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM group_tiles WHERE tile_id = OLD.tile_id)
    BEGIN
        DELETE FROM tiles WHERE id = OLD.tile_id;
    END;
    -- Deletion of any existing orphaned NULL tiles, resources, and patches
    DELETE FROM tiles
        WHERE bytes IS NULL
        AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM patches WHERE tile_id = tiles.id)
        AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM group_tiles WHERE tile_id = tiles.id);
    DELETE FROM resources
        WHERE bytes IS NULL
        AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM group_resources WHERE resource_id = resources.id);
    DELETE FROM patches
        WHERE bytes IS NULL
        AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM group_patches WHERE patch_id = patches.id);
    PRAGMA user_version = 655366;
